<div class="content page-content has-main-media has-main-image tmpl-loading" data-url-id="header-alt" id="header-alt-page"><article class="entry h-entry hentry tag-pamela-des-barres author-brendan-mcdonald post-type-text article-index-1" data-item-id="5db0f90809eaa25c48d458cc" id="article-5db0f90809eaa25c48d458cc">
<header class="entry-header">
<div class="meta-above-title"><div class="episode-number">Episode 1065</div>
<div class="entry-dateline">
<time class="dt-published published entry-date" datetime="2019-10-24" pubdate=""><a class="entry-dateline-link" href="/podcast/episode-1065-pamela-des-barres">October 24, 2019</a></time>
<time class="dt-updated updated" datetime="2019-10-24"></time>
</div>
<div class="entry-byline">
<span class="entry-author vcard"><a class="p-author author url fn entry-byline-link" href="/podcast?author=5706c6d0b654f954ba063961" rel="author">Brendan McDonald</a></span>
</div>
</div>
<h1 class="entry-title entry-title-list p-name" data-content-field="title">
<a class="u-url" href="/podcast/episode-1065-pamela-des-barres" rel="bookmark">Pamela Des Barres</a>
</h1>
<div class="meta-below-title">
<div class="entry-dateline">
<time class="dt-published published entry-date" datetime="2019-10-24" pubdate=""><a class="entry-dateline-link" href="/podcast/episode-1065-pamela-des-barres">October 24, 2019</a></time>
<time class="dt-updated updated" datetime="2019-10-24"></time>
</div>
<div class="entry-byline">
<span class="entry-author vcard"><a class="p-author author url fn entry-byline-link" href="/podcast?author=5706c6d0b654f954ba063961" rel="author">Brendan McDonald</a></span>
</div>
</div>
</header>
<div class="entry-content">
<div class="e-content"><div class="sqs-layout sqs-grid-12 columns-12" data-layout-label="Post Body" data-type="item" data-updated-on="1571879937338" id="item-5db0f90809eaa25c48d458cc"><div class="row sqs-row"><div class="col sqs-col-12 span-12"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" id="block-6c845a20494b796d1a0f"><div class="sqs-block-content"><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">No one can doubt Pamela Des Barres’s commitment to the life of rock and roll. She’s known as THE rock groupie, but further distinguished herself as a writer, educator, tour guide and interviewer, all involving her life on the road throughout modern music history. Growing up in California with a love of Jesus and Elvis, it wasn’t surprising she was drawn to the charismatic allure of rock stars. Pamela tells Marc about her time with Frank Zappa, Phil Spector, Jimmy Page, Robert Plant, Keith Moon, Mick Jagger, Tiny Tim, Jim Morrison, Waylon Jennings, and more, as she experienced the highs of the Free Love 60s as well as the era’s dismal end at Altamont. This episode is sponsored by <a href="http://zoro.com/wtf">Zoro.com</a> and <a href="http://newmexico.org">New Mexico Tourism &amp; Travel</a>.</p></div></div><div class="sqs-block audio-block sqs-block-audio" data-block-type="41" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1571878992891_90999"><div class="sqs-block-content">
<div class="sqs-audio-embed" data-author="" data-color-theme="dark" data-design-style="minimal" data-duration-in-ms="" data-mime-type="" data-show-download="true" data-title="Episode 1065 - Pamela Des Barres" data-url="http://traffic.libsyn.com/wtfpod/WTF_-_EPISODE_1065_PAMELA_DES_BARRES.mp3">
</div>
</div></div></div></div></div></div>
</div>

<div class="intrinsic" id="yui_3_17_2_1_1572140310279_78" style="max-width:1053.0px;">

<h1 class="entry-title entry-title-list p-name" data-content-field="title">
<a class="u-url" href="/dispatches/the-history-of-country" rel="bookmark">The History of Country.</a>
</h1>
<div class="meta-below-title">

<h1 class="entry-title entry-title-list p-name" data-content-field="title">
